# Graph Report - fixture  (2026-11-14)

## Corpus Check
- 4768 files · ~10,849,326 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 108 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 33, .css 17, .entitlements 10)

## Summary
- 62704 nodes · 184733 edges · 1577 communities (988 shown, 589 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6112 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `ac8d6477`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
